# Thirty-fourth-hour research: central quotients, coherent frames, and entire cohomology spaces

Main A, Main B, and the actual full-GT CM parity remain Open. The eight proposed formal statements A.106--A.111 and B.165--B.166 have complete proofs with their actual groups, topology, comparison maps, and precise residual conditions. Central integration requires their final-byte independent reviews; completed build, visual inspection, and immutable archive verification are recorded separately, only after those actions occur.

## Actual progress on Main A

A.106 puts the same canonical marked hexagon H_d in N_1=closure([N,F]), where N is the kernel of the original signed congruence representation. It proves the exact formula N_1=N intersect F' intersect pi_2^{-1}(closure([M_2,F^(2)])), with M_2 the actual pro-two congruence kernel. The proof retains the dyadic image, its possibly nonzero cohomology, and the entire pro-two vanishing of this same H_d. It does not replace the compact image by an abstract completion or identify the resulting closed image with the full coordinate product.

A.107 proves that the prescribed endomorphism is an automorphism on every actual relative-central quotient F/N_j and on their inverse limit F/N_infinity. Full surjectivity is exactly surjectivity on N_infinity. It also proves a genuine modular automorphism and the actual involution conjugacy on B/N_1: the cubic relation is established before the modular homomorphism is defined. No higher cubic relation or cofinality of this tower is inferred. The full signed kernel is geometrically stable, whereas the kernel of q_3 on all of F is not; the modulo-four parity calculation supplies the distinction.

A.108 constructs an actual order-three factor transporter in this first central quotient. The branch is r or s r^{-1} s according to d modulo three. Its abelian vector and its integral signed matrix equation are fixed. The argument uses the central pro-two kernel, an elementary coprime-action proof, and determinant corrections at every odd prime; at three the determinant is already one modulo three, so a scalar square root suffices. The correcting matrices are then placed in the actual derived image. This is existence of a quotient frame, with no uniqueness or full lifting assertion.

A.109 computes the entire nonabelian H^1(C_3,K'_3) for the actual derived kernel K'_3=ker(q_3|F'): it has precisely two points. The proof first computes H^1(C_3,F') and excludes the inverse-torsion branch by its impossible three-adic abelian equation. The original frame obstruction is identified with one of these two points, with label equal to the original CM sign for full-GT data. Every actual equivariant finite p-group quotient for p different from three kills both classes. This last assertion concerns the transporter equation alone and does not exclude a coupled pentagon target.

A.110 proves that the identity automorphism of B/N_1 has two equally normalized pure r-frames whose literal original E.65 recipes give opposite parities. The continuous fixed element is lifted from an explicitly supported three-adic quaternion commutator through the central pro-two kernel. The complete original E.61/E.65 sources and referee discussion were reread at the exact uniqueness, multiplication, and transport steps. The result refutes the use of arbitrary quotient frames for that recipe; it does not refute the original full-group character, exclude a construction with additional coherent framing, or assign the actual full-GT parity. Its nonlifting cocycle also proves that N_1 is not pro-two.

A.111 constructs a choice-independent continuous C_2-cohomology class for coherence of the quotient r-frame with the involution. Its triviality is exactly the existence of a frame h satisfying alpha(h)J=vh, using the ORIGINAL full-group negative conjugator J in each branch. The signed reduction of this class is exactly (-1)^Delta(alpha). Thus the original CM sign is an image of an intrinsic coherent-frame obstruction even though the arbitrary quotient-frame recipe fails. A zero scalar image is not asserted to imply that the entire class is zero.

The next full-group attack is to test the actual applicability of the closed-normal central lifting criterion to this peripheral modular automorphism, with all multiplier powers typed explicitly. Any reduction by a Galois multiplier must prove preservation of the actual signed congruence kernel; conjugacy-only lifting cannot silently be applied to arbitrary powers. A positive lift would still leave the negative involution norm and coherent full-frame conditions to be solved. The two-point C_3 obstruction and the C_2 coherence class provide exact targets for that next step.

## Actual progress on Main B

B.165 computes the whole first cohomology of the actual deletion-kernel closure as the completed source coset space. Every deletion cocycle becomes a coboundary in the actual completed preimage: the finite-factor torsion argument has two possible branches, and the unwanted branch is excluded by a character of the actual FILLED target. The proof retains all nodes and signed source translates. The map from filling-kernel cohomology onto this whole coset space has a continuous pointed section. This is a splitting of pointed compact spaces, not of groups, and does not compute the basepoint fiber.

B.166 computes the entire filling-kernel cohomology as a LEFT-coset space of the actual completed fixed subgroup. Its map to the deletion coset space takes the inverse source coset; two literal section calculations check that convention. The actual contact source is an exact closed subspace. Equality of that subspace with the whole space is equivalent to the remaining local contact-image equality. No such equality is asserted, and the further global icosahedral comparison remains separate. The next attack is the actual basepoint fiber, with all three contact orbits retained.

## Thirty-fifth-hour research: the full residual kernel and compact contact families

### State of the two main decisions

INCOMPLETE. Main A, Main B, and the original full-GT CM parity remain Open. The ten new statements A.112–A.119 and B.167–B.168 sharpen the actual full-group obstruction and determine several entire compact parameter spaces. None replaces the original marked equations by a freely chosen quotient frame, and none promotes a finite-image calculation to a full existence assertion.

### Actual central product and its marked cohomology

A.112 identifies the first relative central congruence quotient with its actual dyadic factor and odd product, including the prime-three fiber-product relation. Its derived subgroup, order-three centralizers, and odd involutory norm coordinates are explicit. The odd part of the relevant involution cohomology is the product of one sign at each odd prime. At three the sign is still the original CM coordinate, whose value has not been determined. The matrix calculation retains the signed cyclic covariance, the trace-minus-one condition, and the original sum character. Beyl's Proposition 5.7 is cited at its precise previously verified scope (Beyl, 1986).

A.113 computes the entire cyclic cohomology of the first relative central kernel by the actual connecting map from the quotient centralizer. The compact-space proof uses complete vanishing upstairs, actual lift sets, and the coprime order-three correction in the pro-two kernel; it does not assume a continuous choice of lifts. A central scalar supported at the prime three changes a quotient negative frame, preserves its displayed negative-frame identities, and flips precisely the prime-three cohomology coordinate. This is a fixed-point-free unpointed symmetry of the quotient classification. The unique original full negative frame is unchanged.

### Modular central tower and a nontrivial residual kernel

A.114 proves the odd-primary second-cohomology vanishing needed for the next modular central refinement. It treats the prime-three root lattice and the alternating term explicitly, identifies the exact next kernel, and proves that the same original hexagon lies there. It then constructs the genuine quotient modular automorphism and applies the closed-normal lifting criterion with the actual multiplier. The full peripheral lift is an existence result with a prescribed quotient action; it is not identified with the canonical full substitution.

A.115 extends these calculations through the entire modular relative central tower. Unique splittings of the odd product across central pro-two kernels identify the actual odd image at every stage. The original hexagon belongs to every tower kernel. The finite-stage full peripheral lifts form nested nonempty closed subsets of the compact automorphism group, so one full lift realizes all prescribed quotient actions simultaneously. This argument does not show that the tower detects every element.

A.116 determines a concrete residual subgroup

\[
R=N_1\cap O=N\cap F'\cap O,
\qquad F/R=P\times D_o,
\qquad R=[R,B].
\]

Here (P) is the full free pro-two quotient, and (D_o) is the actual odd factor with its prime-three relation. Primary-by-primary comparison proves the required second-cohomology vanishing for (B/R). The original hexagon belongs to (R), and the prescribed whole quotient action admits a full peripheral lift. Since (N_1/R) is pro-two whereas (N_1) is not, (R) is nontrivial and is not pro-two. Relative perfectness places (R) inside every modular tower kernel. Thus this tower is proved noncofinal. We do not assert (R=R'), identify (R) with the whole tower intersection, or deduce that the original hexagon is trivial.

### The original cubic condition and normalized residual pentagon

A.117 computes the entire order-three cohomology of (R) by the actual centralizer in (F/R), including its comparison with the first central quotient. The original negative frame is retained in the coherent involution cocycle, and the odd obstruction coordinates are unchanged. Lifting a specified coherent frame across the remaining pro-two kernel still requires its own involutory norm equation.

There is also an exact condition before a proposed full lift can even define a cyclic cohomology class. If (eta=v\delta^d), (h) is a full pure lift of an actual quotient factor frame, and (a_h=h^{-1}\eta hR_e^{-1}in R), then

\[
a_h\rho_e(a_h)\rho_e^2(a_h)
 =h^{-1}u^{-1}H_d^{-1}uh.
\]

Consequently this specified element is a (C_3)-cocycle exactly when the original full word (H_d) is one. The whole-space classification cannot be applied to that element before this equality is proved. Even after the cubic equality, the specified frame lifts to a full factor conjugator only if its resulting class is the basepoint.

A.118 uses the actual full peripheral lift to normalize the negative involution. The unique residual correction lies in (R), and its full dihedral unit is one. The resulting normalization belongs to the stated full \(\widehat{\mathrm{GT}}_0\) scope; full GT membership is not asserted. Evaluating the original full pentagon at the five explicitly marked pure four-strand pairs gives an exact transported identity. Every factor contributed by the normalizing automorphism remains in that identity. We neither give the residual correction an ordinary pentagon by cancellation nor assume that the normalizing automorphism acts on the four-strand group.

### Entire involution cohomology of the residual kernel

A.119 proves, for the standard, original, and normalized involutions at the specified scope, that the whole residual cohomology space has the following pointed compact parameterization:

\[
H^1(C_2,R;a)\cong\prod_{p\text{ odd}}\mathbb Z_p^{\times}.
\]

The proof uses the accepted original LM4 D.34 three-class theorem with its complete proof and referee audit. The three actual involutions have distinct mod-two abelian labels and exhaust the section classes. An (F')-valued cocycle excludes the two nonzero labels; torsion-freeness of the abelianization then puts the remaining conjugator in (F'). Thus the entire cohomology upstairs is trivial. Actual compact lift sets identify the residual cohomology with the quotient fixed space.

For the standard action, the pure pro-two semidirect product is explicitly the free pro-two product of three order-two factors. The relevant factor-centralizer theorem applies with the finite-two-group class; its hypotheses and whole proof were freshly read in the primary text (Herfort & Ribes, 1985, §2 and §4). The odd fixed matrices are exactly diagonal unit tori, with zero odd abelian coordinate. The comparison between the original and normalized actions uses the specified full lift; the standard-to-normalized identification is only the printed identification through their common quotient fixed space. This does not supply an intertwining automorphism of the full residual group or evaluate the original coherent-frame class.

### Whole compact contact family and a strict enlargement

B.167 treats the entire compact (m)-orbit of the contact (D_s). The kernel of the actual free profinite two-generator subgroup over its (m)-coordinate is the free profinite group on the continuously parameterized family (m^zD_sm^{-z}), (z\in\widehat{\mathbb Z}). Explicit inverse universal maps prove the semidirect-product description. The whole family embeds into the actual contact image, using the already established full topology and an explicit source retraction. No free-factor or retraction claim for the whole contact image is made.

The abstract completion of the countable discrete orbit kernel maps onto this compact family but is not injective. A character supported at the zeroth discrete orbit generator detects a word that every ambient finite image identifies periodically. This distinguishes the full topology of the containing rank-two subgroup from the finer abstract topology of its discrete kernel.

B.168 gives a strict enlargement: with the printed convention ([x,y]=xyx^{-1}y^{-1}),

\[
r=[m,B_sD_sB_s^{-1}]
\]

belongs to the actual contact image but lies outside that entire compact single-orbit subgroup. The actual ordinary regional presentation maps to a free-seven fiber semidirect its two-generator source. A prescribed (S_4) fiber map sends (r) to the three-cycle ((124)) and sends the compact (D_s)-orbit into the cyclic subgroup generated by ((34)). An explicit characteristic finite-index core extends this fiber map to a genuine finite quotient of the full semidirect group. The argument does not pretend that the original small fiber map itself is source-equivariant.

The complete checker, actual exit-zero output, and certificate were read. It recomputes the raw contact words and signed source operators from the frozen original data, verifies the free-basis changes, and evaluates the specified permutations. The characteristic-core extension is proved, not claimed to have been enumerated. This separates two contact subspaces; (r) is already inside the contact image, so it is not a counterexample to Main B.

## Thirty-sixth-hour research: elliptic torsion and complementary contact quotients

### Exact decision status

Main A, Main B, the original coherent involution class and the actual CM parity remain **Open**. This cycle adds eleven proved statements at their explicit scopes. Conditional conclusions retain their hypotheses. No finite quotient classification is promoted to an existence assertion in the full profinite group.

### Forgetting odd centralizer coordinates

A120 computes the actual inclusion-induced map from coherent centralizer cohomology to the full residual derived product. Every odd local coordinate is killed, including at three, by an integral determinant-corrected cocycle argument. The remaining ambient pointed space is the dyadic one. This does not evaluate the specified original coherent class: forgetting the centralizer permits changes of the positive factor frame.

### The exact elliptic cubic and the original boundary obstruction

A121 uses the full profinite section of Enriquez (2014, Proposition 2.20), applied only to established full-GT elements. Its separate ordered calculation proves a marked order-three identity from the original pentagon alone. The transported version retains every normalizing factor and every transformed pair. It supplies no elliptic action to an unproved GT pair or a merely GT-zero lift.

A122 embeds the original free pair as a literal free factor of an open index-two subgroup of the punctured-torus group. It identifies the original specified third-cusp conjugacy, the elliptic boundary-power equation, and the intertwining identity as equivalent conditions. Their exact normal obstruction is invariant under the original elliptic involution. These equivalent conditions remain unproved.

A123 proves that an endomorphism of the full free profinite rank-two group carrying the boundary commutator to a unit power is an automorphism. The proof uses actual finite punctured-torus covers and their integral boundary homology. For the candidate of A122, the still-unproved boundary equation would also imply automorphy of the original normalized map on the actual embedded free factor. No pro-p transfer or unverified discrete-retract assertion is used.

### Actual reduced elliptic torsion and unique derived transporters

A124 identifies the actual reduced three-strand elliptic braid group as the lift/orbifold group of the normalized torus configuration space. Its pure subgroup is free-three by free-two, with the full induced topology and all required goodness and cohomological-type hypotheses verified. An ordinary cellular cohomology argument gives a unique fixed vertex for each order-three subgroup of the lift group and excludes order two. The precise prime-order theorem and proof of Boggi and Zalesskii transfer the classification and centralizers to the full completion. There are four nontrivial finite-subgroup classes, all cyclic of order three, and eight nonidentity torsion-element classes distinguished by their nonzero abelian labels.

Each actual pentagon cubic therefore has a unique pure transporter from the fixed base cubic, continuously depending on the word. The actual elliptic involution gives a specified nonabelian C2 class and an actual commuting C6 extension. Constancy of that class does not prove it trivial or identify it with the original coherent CM frame.

A125 computes the actual pure integral abelianization as Z^4 using the relative homology of the three deleted subtori. The cyclic action has determinant nine after subtracting the identity and hence no full profinite invariant vector. This forces the unique transporter, and then the specified involution cocycle, into the closed pure derived subgroup. The entire C3 cohomology of that derived subgroup is trivial. The refined C2 class remains unevaluated; injectivity into the larger C2 cohomology space is not asserted.

### Whole one-sided and complementary contact images

B169 proves the full induced topology of the entire one-sided five-generator contact image by refining every finite target through actual whole-source maps. Its kernel is an exact continuous free profinite family, and the actual contact kernel maps onto it. B170 gives a literal continuous section and identifies the entire normal defect as the closed normal closure of the specified noncommuting contact. The section's exact action correction prevents an equivariance claim.

B171 proves that the two mirrored deletions have identical restrictions on the actual filled fixed source and its compact closure, including every finite refinement. They do not reduce the defect jointly. B172 uses a different actual retained map to show that the defect has an image containing the entire derived subgroup of a free profinite group of rank two; consequently every finite two-generated perfect group is a quotient.

B173 determines the full topology, exact normal kernel and continuous section of a complementary retained two-color contact target. Its proof separately extends every finite restriction through the original whole source, without asserting an unavailable signed action on the target quotient. It detects the first target's discarded defect and kills the mixed defect detected by that first target. The complete profinite family of normal translates identifies its retained image of the first defect. No original presentation of the full source or Main B fixed-generation equality follows.

### Sources, preservation and next work

All eleven entire formal proofs and their complete independent reviews were read before integration. The new Enriquez bibliography entry records the actual section/presentation/action proof scope, not an entire-paper read.
